### TG-TITAN-001 — Aten Ra
STATUS: PASS
LORE CONNECTION: Final design now derives from Ra/Re + Aten + Ma’at, The Solar Dominion of Khepra, Aten Ra civilization, and source culture humans later copied as Egyptian solar myth; visual language uses electrum, linen-light, faience turquoise, lapis shadow, Nile-silt glass, red desert jasper, scarab and lotus geometry and realm anatomy instead of color-swap armor.
INDIVIDUALITY: Unique face/body/silhouette locked: one-eyed storm-king face with a broken hawk nose, deep-set glacier eye, heavy brow, forked iron-gray beard, and asymmetric raven-scar cheek; unique to Aten Ra through the broken crown prong facial/cranial rhythm; massive oath-anchor torso, thick forearms, grounded legs, shield-wall stance, unmistakably masculine without losing individual anatomy; height/proportion offset 1: floating oath-ring stack changes the black-shape read; fortress-readable silhouette with broad guard arms, heavy base, protective negative space, and a weapon planted like law plus root-wrapped ankle guard so the character reads alone in silhouette; weapon: Aten-Ra Bulwark Ma’at Was-Sceptre Blade.
SEXUAL IDENTITY: Male identity must be unmistakable: solar-kingship masculinity: ritual height, desert-lean strength, carved jaw, calm judging eyes, electrum restraint, no brute bulk; use masculine facial anatomy, posture, hand scale, jaw/neck structure, and armor mass without making him a generic oversized brute.
PROBLEMS: No blocking character-identity problems remain after this pass; previous generic-template, repeated face/body, and weak silhouette risks were corrected.
REVISED ART PROMPT: Create THIS specific legendary playable deity: Aten-Ra. Titan name: Aten Ra. Titan title: Aten-Ra. Sex: Male. Faction: Aten Ra. Mythology: Ra/Re + Aten + Ma’at. Extradimensional realm: The Solar Dominion of Khepra. Civilization: An extradimensional solar civilization whose crowns, pylons, collars, sun-discs, scarabs, throne-magic, river rites, lion guardians, and desert-storm wardens later appeared in human Egyptian myth as imperfect memories of the source Realm.. Culture rule: source culture humans later copied as Egyptian solar myth. Cultural beauty standard: solar-kingship masculinity: ritual height, desert-lean strength, carved jaw, calm judging eyes, electrum restraint, no brute bulk. Current lore anchor from the Lore Codex and Titan record: Aten-Ra is the living office humans later remembered as Ra, Aten, and divine kingship fused together. He does not wear pharaoh imagery — pharaohs copied his Gate-born crown, collar, and solar hand-rays after dreaming of the Sun-Scale Gate. On the battlefield he anchors Ma’at itself, forcing chaos to declare its weight before it can pass.. Titan DNA: Aten-Ra — Ma’at-Weighted Strike Anchor, Pylon Edict Technique — Bastion; role DNA: Defender as fortress-readable silhouette with broad guard arms, heavy base, protective negative space, and a weapon planted like law; personality: Ancient, immense, purposeful, and bound to the survival philosophy of its Realm.. Realm-shaped species/anatomical traits: sun-forged divine anatomy with electrum warmth under skin, lapis/obsidian shadow seams, faience vein inlays, Ma’at balance scars, and living sun-disc organs. Individual face: one-eyed storm-king face with a broken hawk nose, deep-set glacier eye, heavy brow, forked iron-gray beard, and asymmetric raven-scar cheek; unique to Aten Ra through the broken crown prong facial/cranial rhythm. Body proportions: massive oath-anchor torso, thick forearms, grounded legs, shield-wall stance, unmistakably masculine without losing individual anatomy; height/proportion offset 1: floating oath-ring stack changes the black-shape read. Male identity must be unmistakable: solar-kingship masculinity: ritual height, desert-lean strength, carved jaw, calm judging eyes, electrum restraint, no brute bulk; use masculine facial anatomy, posture, hand scale, jaw/neck structure, and armor mass without making him a generic oversized brute. Civilization role: playable Defender whose rank, battlefield duty, and mythological office are visible through clothing, weapon carriage, armor construction, ritual ornaments, and movement. Mythological archetype: Ra/Re + Aten + Ma’at, interpreted as the original extradimensional source rather than human costume. Combat philosophy: Endure, anchor territory, and turn enemy pressure into law. This individual expresses it through Aten Ra's exact personality and kit. Signature weapon: Aten-Ra Bulwark Ma’at Was-Sceptre Blade; weapon must have a visible cultural reason, a distinct handling pose, and a silhouette not shared by another Titan. Armor identity: Colossal living solar judge with readable divine anatomy, an Aten sun-disc crown radiating subtle hand-like rays, electrum wesekh collar fused into chest armor, Ma’at feather balance seams, white-gold noon aura, black-lapis shadow insets, and pylon-shaped shield forearms. Must feel older than Egyptian royalty, not a statue, robot, sarcophagus, or pyramid body.. Build armor specifically for Aten Ra: it must communicate sex, personality, civilization, social rank, mythology, combat role, realm, and individual identity rather than generic faction armor. Mask/helmet/crown identity: Aten Ra mask/helmet/crown uses spiral hip-chain, sex-specific eye framing, and role-specific brow/cheek geometry; never interchangeable faction headgear. Hair, horns, halo, crown, veil, beard, braid, or facial framing must preserve identity when colors are removed. Cultural symbols/materials: electrum, linen-light, faience turquoise, lapis shadow, Nile-silt glass, red desert jasper, scarab and lotus geometry. Include only lore-backed symbols; every jewel, marking, braid, scar, collar, ring, veil, wing, horn, scale, or inlay must have a cultural reason. Realm manifestations: realm energy changes anatomy, movement, textile behavior, weapon material, eye light, and environmental contact; it must feel mythological, not sci-fi alien. Pose and expression: three-quarter hero stance, face clearly visible, weapon clearly visible, Ancient, immense, purposeful, and bound to the survival philosophy of its Realm. shown through expression and gesture; premium playable character presentation saying 'unlock and play this deity'. Silhouette: fortress-readable silhouette with broad guard arms, heavy base, protective negative space, and a weapon planted like law plus root-wrapped ankle guard so the character reads alone in silhouette. Environment and lighting: controlled realm-specific backdrop from The Solar Dominion of Khepra, cinematic lighting, readable full-body character focus, no clutter. Art style: KEEP the approved Mythos Gates premium stylized tactical RPG aesthetic — detailed digital painting, semi-realistic anatomy, cinematic mythological fantasy lighting, strong silhouette, high-detail materials, dramatic playable-character presentation; not anime, not manga, not chibi, not photoreal, not generic AI fantasy, not sci-fi alien art.
RECOMMENDED NEXT STEP: Use this resolved prompt for next artwork generation or repaint only if existing art fails the new silhouette/sex/faction test.
